<!-- EARTHLINE 16338 — REMOVE DELAYED PROPERTY AQUIFER PLACEMENT WITHOUT CHANGING SCIENCE.

Parent SHA-256: d78023818dfefc54e530b37587f37c35a78cd495b41e619236fe23f2f0ff59eb.

CONNECTED DEFECT OBSERVED 2026-08-11:
After Property corridors and modeled recharge targets were already visible, verified aquifer
context could arrive noticeably later. The Property result itself was otherwise visually good.

ROOT CAUSE / REPAIR:
A Property evidence request could be suppressed by M.usgsAquiferLoading while an older
Regional aquifer request was still in flight. That older request had no latest-request ownership
guard, so the current Property request could wait until the old request finished before the
correct local aquifer request began.

16338 keeps the existing pre-science behavior unchanged, but once Property science has
already completed it allows the current Property aquifer request to supersede a stale in-flight
request from a different bbox. Aquifer requests are keyed by bbox and latest-request owned so
an older response cannot overwrite a newer location. Repeated bounded Property evidence
retries deduplicate against the same current bbox instead of launching duplicate requests.
Already-loaded official aquifer context is reused immediately only when its recorded bbox
contains the current Property bbox; that valid context is explicitly re-synced at Property
publication. The normal post-terminal retries remain as resilience.

function earthlinePropertyAquiferQueryBBox16391(site){
  const pts=Array.isArray(site&&site.analysisBoundaryLngLat)?site.analysisBoundaryLngLat:[];
  let minLng=Infinity,minLat=Infinity,maxLng=-Infinity,maxLat=-Infinity;
  for(const p of pts){
    const x=Number(p&&p[0]),y=Number(p&&p[1]);
    if(!Number.isFinite(x)||!Number.isFinite(y))continue;
    minLng=Math.min(minLng,x);maxLng=Math.max(maxLng,x);minLat=Math.min(minLat,y);maxLat=Math.max(maxLat,y);
  }
  const cx=Number(site&&site.lng),cy=Number(site&&site.lat);
  if(!Number.isFinite(minLng)||!Number.isFinite(minLat)||!Number.isFinite(maxLng)||!Number.isFinite(maxLat)){
    if(!Number.isFinite(cx)||!Number.isFinite(cy))return null;
    minLng=maxLng=cx;minLat=maxLat=cy;
  }
  const midLat=Number.isFinite(cy)?cy:(minLat+maxLat)/2;
  const cos=Math.max(.35,Math.cos(midLat*Math.PI/180));
  const spanLng=Math.max(0,maxLng-minLng),spanLat=Math.max(0,maxLat-minLat);
  /* Property science is local, but mapped aquifer context is regional. Query a stable
     authoritative context window around the selected Property rather than the camera
     position that may still belong to the previous search. No geometry is invented. */
  const padLat=Math.max(.12,spanLat*4);
  const padLng=Math.max(.12/cos,spanLng*4);
  return {minLng:minLng-padLng,minLat:minLat-padLat,maxLng:maxLng+padLng,maxLat:maxLat+padLat};
}


/* EARTHLINE 16398 — authoritative Vermont Property groundwater/aquifer resolver.
   The user-supplied coverage index is intentionally NOT used for parcel coverage claims
   because its own meta says geometryAttached=false. Live state geometry + well evidence
   answer the Property request; the national owner remains available to Regional/non-VT. */
function earthlineIsVermontProperty16398(){
  const loc=M&&M.loc||{};const lng=Number(loc.lng),lat=Number(loc.lat);
  const text=String((loc.label||loc.name||loc.query||"")+" "+(loc.state||"")).toLowerCase();
  return (Number.isFinite(lng)&&Number.isFinite(lat)&&lng>=-73.50&&lng<=-71.35&&lat>=42.70&&lat<=45.05)||/\bvermont\b|\bvt\b/.test(text);
}
function earthlineExactPropertyBBox16398(){
  const pts=Array.isArray(M&&M.analysisBoundaryLngLat)?M.analysisBoundaryLngLat:[];
  let minLng=Infinity,minLat=Infinity,maxLng=-Infinity,maxLat=-Infinity;

  const loc=M&&M.loc||{},cx=Number(loc.lng),cy=Number(loc.lat);
  if(!Number.isFinite(minLng)||!Number.isFinite(minLat)||!Number.isFinite(maxLng)||!Number.isFinite(maxLat)){

}
function earthlineExpandBBoxM16398(bb,meters){
  const cy=(bb.minLat+bb.maxLat)/2,cos=Math.max(.25,Math.cos(cy*Math.PI/180));
  const dLat=meters/110540,dLng=meters/(111320*cos);
  return {minLng:bb.minLng-dLng,minLat:bb.minLat-dLat,maxLng:bb.maxLng+dLng,maxLat:bb.maxLat+dLat};
}
function earthlineDistanceM16398(a,b){
  const lat=(Number(a[1])+Number(b[1]))*.5*Math.PI/180;
  return Math.hypot((Number(a[0])-Number(b[0]))*111320*Math.cos(lat),(Number(a[1])-Number(b[1]))*110540);
}
function earthlineMedian16398(vals){
  const a=(vals||[]).map(Number).filter(Number.isFinite).sort((x,y)=>x-y);if(!a.length)return null;const m=Math.floor(a.length/2);return a.length%2?a[m]:(a[m-1]+a[m])/2;
}

function drawBoundary(){

function drawMeasure(){

  ctx.setLineDash([]);
  for(const[x,y]of pts){ctx.beginPath();ctx.arc(x,y,4,0,7);ctx.fill();}
  if(pts.length===2){
    const distM=Math.hypot(pts[1][0]-pts[0][0],pts[1][1]-pts[0][1])/view.s*M.cellM;
    const lbl=distM>=1000?(distM/1000).toFixed(2)+" km":Math.round(distM)+" m";

    ctx.fillStyle="#ffd1ec";ctx.fillText(lbl,mx,my);
  }
  ctx.restore();
}

function render(navigationCoreOnly=false){
  // BUILD-CHECK-15805 — ONE AUTHORITATIVE LAYER OWNER.
  // With live Mapbox, water paths, bioswales, recharge targets, elevation
  // contours and aquifer context are owned only by geographic Mapbox layers.
  // The analytical canvas cannot draw those responsibilities.
  if(window.earthlineAnalyticalCanvasValid15783===false){
    earthlineClearOverlay();
    if(cv)cv.style.visibility="hidden";
    return;
  }
  const dpr=window.devicePixelRatio||1;
  ctx.setTransform(dpr,0,0,dpr,0,0);
  const w=cv.width/dpr,h=cv.height/dpr;
  const nativeMap=earthlineMapActive();
  if(nativeMap)ctx.clearRect(0,0,w,h);
  else{ctx.fillStyle="#05080d";ctx.fillRect(0,0,w,h);}
  ctx.save();ctx.beginPath();ctx.rect(0,0,w,h);ctx.clip();
  if(terrainImg&&!nativeMap){
    ctx.imageSmoothingEnabled=true;
    const pad=2*view.s;
    ctx.drawImage(terrainImg,view.ox-pad,view.oy-pad,GW*view.s+pad*2,GH*view.s+pad*2);
  }
  const thr=streamThreshold();
  const L=M.layers;
  const regional=earthlineLargeAreaMode()||earthlinePlanningMode();
  const analysisVisible=earthlinePropertyAnalysisVisible();
  earthlineUseFastPropertyProjection=!!(nativeMap&&!regional&&analysisVisible);
  const detail=!regional&&(swaleDetailScale()||analysisVisible);

  if(!navigationCoreOnly&&L.soil.on&&detail)drawSoil();
  if(!navigationCoreOnly&&L.landcover.on&&detail)drawLandcover();

  if(nativeMap){
    earthlineUpdateRechargeSvg(false);
    if(typeof window.earthlineApplyRendererOwnership15805==="function"){
      window.earthlineApplyRendererOwnership15805(regional?"regional":"property");
    }
  }else{
    // Offline/no-Mapbox fallback has one canvas owner.
    if(L.recharge.on&&!regional&&detail)drawRecharge();
    if(L.usgsAquifer.on)drawUSGSAquifers();
    if(!navigationCoreOnly&&L.contours.on&&detail)drawContours();
    if(detail&&L.flow.on)drawFlow(thr);
    if(!regional&&L.swales.on&&(analysisVisible||swaleDetailScale()))drawSwales();
    if(!navigationCoreOnly&&detail&&L.arrows.on&&L.flow.on)drawArrows(thr);
    if(!navigationCoreOnly&&L.contours.on&&detail)drawContourTopOverlay();
    if(regional&&L.swales.on)drawRegionalPriority(thr);
    if(regional)drawRegionalPoliticalFrame();
  }

  if(!navigationCoreOnly&&L.fema.on&&detail)drawPonds();
  if(!navigationCoreOnly&&detail&&L.erosion.on)drawErosion(thr);
  if(!navigationCoreOnly&&detail&&L.noBuild&&L.noBuild.on)drawNoBuildMask();
  if(detail&&L.boundary.on&&M.boundary&&M.boundary.length&&M.boundarySource!=="analysis-window")drawBoundary();
  if(!regional)drawSiteMarker();
  drawMeasure();
  drawUserDrawings();
  ctx.restore();
}

function earthlineLocIsLargeArea(loc){
  if(!loc||!Array.isArray(loc.bbox)||loc.bbox.length!==4)return false;
  const spanDeg=Math.max(Math.abs(loc.bbox[2]-loc.bbox[0]),Math.abs(loc.bbox[3]-loc.bbox[1]));
  return spanDeg>1;
}

  if(earthlineMapActive()&&earthlineMap.getCenter){
    const c=earthlineMap.getCenter();
    if(c&&Number.isFinite(c.lng)&&Number.isFinite(c.lat))return [c.lng,c.lat];

function earthlinePreparePlanningAquiferView(loc=M.loc){
  if(!loc||!M.layers||!M.layers.usgsAquifer)return;
  const planningView=earthlinePlanningMode(loc)||earthlineLocIsLargeArea(loc);
  if(!planningView)return;
  // Official aquifer context is a regional/city feature and must not be disabled merely
  // because the searched geography is large. Parcel recharge remains analysis-only.
  M.layers.usgsAquifer.on=true;
  setTimeout(()=>{
    if(M.loc!==loc)return;
    if(MB_ON())fetchUSGSAquifers();
    render();
  },80);
}
async function runSearch(options={}){
  const autoAnalyze=!!(options&&options.autoAnalyze===true);
  const input=document.getElementById("searchInput");
  let q=input?input.value:"";
  const blankRequest=!q.trim();
  // The primary action must always do useful work. With a blank field, use the
  // current/default Earthline location directly and skip an unnecessary geocoder call.
  let loc=null;
  if(blankRequest){
    loc=M.loc||GAZ[0];
    q=loc.fullName||((loc.name||"")+(loc.sub?", "+loc.sub:""));
    if(input)input.value=q;
  }
  const myGen=++M.searchGen;
  M.terrainReady=false;M.analysisReady=false;
  earthlineProgress("geocode");
  if(!loc)loc=earthlineRegionOverride(q);
  if(MB_ON()&&!loc){
    try{loc=await earthlineTimed("geocoding",()=>geocodeMapbox(q,myGen));}catch(e){loc=null;}
  }
  if(earthlineIsStale(myGen))return false;
  if(!loc)loc=findLoc(q);
  if(!loc){earthlineReady(MB_ON()?"No result — try a fuller address.":"Offline mode: choose a suggested site.");return false;}

  const originalLoc=loc;
  const planningRequest=autoAnalyze&&(earthlinePlanningMode(loc)||earthlineLocIsLargeArea(loc));
  const ok=await applyLocation(loc,null,myGen,{analyzeNow:autoAnalyze&&!planningRequest});
  if(!ok||earthlineIsStale(myGen))return false;
  earthlinePreparePlanningAquiferView(loc);
  M.appliedSearchText=earthlineSearchKey(q);
  addRecentSearch(originalLoc,q);
  if(planningRequest){
    earthlineActivateSelectSiteMode("City planning view ready — click the area you want to evaluate. Earthline will zoom in and run site analysis automatically.");
    toast("Click anywhere on the map to select and analyze a 20-acre site.");
  }
  return true;
}

  function normalizeDistribution15778(values){
    const raw=(values||[]).slice(0,5).map(v=>Math.max(0,Number(v)||0));
    while(raw.length<5)raw.push(0);
    let total=raw.reduce((a,b)=>a+b,0);
    if(total<=0){raw[2]=100;total=100;}
    const p=raw.map(v=>Number((v/total*100).toFixed(1)));
    const diff=Number((100-p.reduce((a,b)=>a+b,0)).toFixed(1));
    p[2]=Number((p[2]+diff).toFixed(1));
    return p;
  }

  const metersPerLng=lat=>111320*Math.max(.05,Math.cos(Number(lat||0)*Math.PI/180));
  const coordDistanceM=(a,b)=>{
    const lat=(Number(a[1])+Number(b[1]))*.5;
    return Math.hypot((Number(b[0])-Number(a[0]))*metersPerLng(lat),(Number(b[1])-Number(a[1]))*111320);
  };
  const lineLengthM=coords=>{
    let d=0;for(let i=1;i<(coords||[]).length;i++)d+=coordDistanceM(coords[i-1],coords[i]);return d;
  };
  const pointSegmentDistanceM=(p,a,b)=>{
    const lat=Number(p[1]);
    const ax=(Number(a[0])-Number(p[0]))*metersPerLng(lat),ay=(Number(a[1])-lat)*111320;
    const bx=(Number(b[0])-Number(p[0]))*metersPerLng(lat),by=(Number(b[1])-lat)*111320;
    const dx=bx-ax,dy=by-ay,den=dx*dx+dy*dy;
    const t=den?Math.max(0,Math.min(1,-(ax*dx+ay*dy)/den)):0;
    return Math.hypot(ax+t*dx,ay+t*dy);
  };
  const pointInRing=(p,ring)=>{
    let inside=false;

    }
    return inside;
  };
  const ringDistanceM=(p,ring)=>{

function parseWKTPolygons(wkt){
  // minimal WKT POLYGON / MULTIPOLYGON ring extractor -> array of [ [lng,lat], ... ]
  const rings=[];
  const re=/\(([-0-9.,\s]+)\)/g;let m;
  while((m=re.exec(wkt))){
    const pts=m[1].trim().split(",").map(pr=>{
      const xy=pr.trim().split(/\s+/).map(Number);
      return [xy[0],xy[1]];
    }).filter(p=>p.length===2&&!isNaN(p[0])&&!isNaN(p[1]));
    if(pts.length>=3)rings.push(pts);
  }
  return rings;
}
// Rock-type -> relative permeability/recharge value (per USGS Ground Water Atlas classes).
// Sand & gravel and karst carbonate transmit water fastest; igneous/metamorphic slowest.
function aquiferPermFromName(name){
  const n=(name||"").toLowerCase();
  if(n.includes("sand and gravel")||n.includes("sand & gravel")||n.includes("basin-fill")||n.includes("allu",0))return 1.0;
  if(n.includes("carbonate")||n.includes("karst")||n.includes("limestone")||n.includes("dolomite"))return 0.92;
  if(n.includes("semiconsolidated")||n.includes("semi-consolidated"))return 0.8;
  if(n.includes("sandstone"))return 0.72;
  if(n.includes("basalt")||n.includes("volcanic"))return 0.6;
  if(n.includes("igneous")||n.includes("metamorphic")||n.includes("crystalline"))return 0.4;
  return 0.7;   // unknown principal aquifer = moderately permeable by default
}
function earthlineAquiferGeoJSON(){
  const features=[];
  (M.usgsAquifers||[]).forEach((poly,i)=>{
    const ring=(poly.lngLatPts||[]).filter(c=>Array.isArray(c)&&Number.isFinite(c[0])&&Number.isFinite(c[1]));
    if(ring.length<3)return;
    const closed=(ring[0][0]===ring[ring.length-1][0]&&ring[0][1]===ring[ring.length-1][1])?ring:ring.concat([ring[0]]);
    features.push({type:"Feature",id:"aq-"+i,properties:{
      featureKind:"aquifer-polygon",
      name:poly.name||"Mapped aquifer",
      source:poly.source||"Official aquifer data",
      aquiferType:poly.aquiferType||"aquifer",
      evidenceScale:poly.evidenceScale||""
    },geometry:{type:"Polygon",coordinates:[closed]}});
  });
  (M.vtGroundwaterWells16398||[]).forEach((w,i)=>{
    const lng=Number(w.lng),lat=Number(w.lat);if(!Number.isFinite(lng)||!Number.isFinite(lat))return;
    features.push({type:"Feature",id:"well-"+i,properties:{
      featureKind:"well",name:"Groundwater well",aquiferType:"vt-well-observation",
      evidence:String(w.evidence||"GROUNDWATER WELL"),distanceM:Number(w.distanceM||0),
      wellType:String(w.wellType||""),yieldGPM:Number.isFinite(Number(w.yieldGPM))?Number(w.yieldGPM):null,
      staticWaterLevel:Number.isFinite(Number(w.staticWaterLevel))?Number(w.staticWaterLevel):null,
      overBurdenThickness:Number.isFinite(Number(w.overBurdenThickness))?Number(w.overBurdenThickness):null,
      source:"VT ANR Private Wells"
    },geometry:{type:"Point",coordinates:[lng,lat]}});
  });
  const summary=M.vtGroundwaterSummaryPoint16398;
  if(summary&&Number.isFinite(Number(summary.lng))&&Number.isFinite(Number(summary.lat))&&summary.label){
    features.push({type:"Feature",id:"vt-groundwater-summary",properties:{
      featureKind:"groundwater-summary",name:String(summary.label),aquiferType:"vt-groundwater-summary",
      source:"VT ANR groundwater evidence"
    },geometry:{type:"Point",coordinates:[Number(summary.lng),Number(summary.lat)]}});
  }
  return {type:"FeatureCollection",features};
}

function earthlinePropertyAquiferQueryBBox16391(site){
  const pts=Array.isArray(site&&site.analysisBoundaryLngLat)?site.analysisBoundaryLngLat:[];
  let minLng=Infinity,minLat=Infinity,maxLng=-Infinity,maxLat=-Infinity;
  for(const p of pts){
    const x=Number(p&&p[0]),y=Number(p&&p[1]);
    if(!Number.isFinite(x)||!Number.isFinite(y))continue;
    minLng=Math.min(minLng,x);maxLng=Math.max(maxLng,x);minLat=Math.min(minLat,y);maxLat=Math.max(maxLat,y);
  }
  const cx=Number(site&&site.lng),cy=Number(site&&site.lat);
  if(!Number.isFinite(minLng)||!Number.isFinite(minLat)||!Number.isFinite(maxLng)||!Number.isFinite(maxLat)){
    if(!Number.isFinite(cx)||!Number.isFinite(cy))return null;
    minLng=maxLng=cx;minLat=maxLat=cy;
  }
  const midLat=Number.isFinite(cy)?cy:(minLat+maxLat)/2;
  const cos=Math.max(.35,Math.cos(midLat*Math.PI/180));
  const spanLng=Math.max(0,maxLng-minLng),spanLat=Math.max(0,maxLat-minLat);
  /* Property science is local, but mapped aquifer context is regional. Query a stable
     authoritative context window around the selected Property rather than the camera
     position that may still belong to the previous search. No geometry is invented. */
  const padLat=Math.max(.12,spanLat*4);
  const padLng=Math.max(.12/cos,spanLng*4);
  return {minLng:minLng-padLng,minLat:minLat-padLat,maxLng:maxLng+padLng,maxLat:maxLat+padLat};
}


/* EARTHLINE 16398 — authoritative Vermont Property groundwater/aquifer resolver.
   The user-supplied coverage index is intentionally NOT used for parcel coverage claims
   because its own meta says geometryAttached=false. Live state geometry + well evidence
   answer the Property request; the national owner remains available to Regional/non-VT. */
function earthlineIsVermontProperty16398(){
  const loc=M&&M.loc||{};const lng=Number(loc.lng),lat=Number(loc.lat);
  const text=String((loc.label||loc.name||loc.query||"")+" "+(loc.state||"")).toLowerCase();
  return (Number.isFinite(lng)&&Number.isFinite(lat)&&lng>=-73.50&&lng<=-71.35&&lat>=42.70&&lat<=45.05)||/\bvermont\b|\bvt\b/.test(text);
}
function earthlineExactPropertyBBox16398(){
  const pts=Array.isArray(M&&M.analysisBoundaryLngLat)?M.analysisBoundaryLngLat:[];
  let minLng=Infinity,minLat=Infinity,maxLng=-Infinity,maxLat=-Infinity;
  for(const p of pts){const x=Number(p&&p[0]),y=Number(p&&p[1]);if(!Number.isFinite(x)||!Number.isFinite(y))continue;minLng=Math.min(minLng,x);maxLng=Math.max(maxLng,x);minLat=Math.min(minLat,y);maxLat=Math.max(maxLat,y);}
  const loc=M&&M.loc||{},cx=Number(loc.lng),cy=Number(loc.lat);
  if(!Number.isFinite(minLng)||!Number.isFinite(minLat)||!Number.isFinite(maxLng)||!Number.isFinite(maxLat)){
    if(!Number.isFinite(cx)||!Number.isFinite(cy))return null;minLng=maxLng=cx;minLat=maxLat=cy;
  }
  return {minLng,minLat,maxLng,maxLat};
}
function earthlineExpandBBoxM16398(bb,meters){
  const cy=(bb.minLat+bb.maxLat)/2,cos=Math.max(.25,Math.cos(cy*Math.PI/180));
  const dLat=meters/110540,dLng=meters/(111320*cos);
  return {minLng:bb.minLng-dLng,minLat:bb.minLat-dLat,maxLng:bb.maxLng+dLng,maxLat:bb.maxLat+dLat};
}
function earthlineDistanceM16398(a,b){
  const lat=(Number(a[1])+Number(b[1]))*.5*Math.PI/180;
  return Math.hypot((Number(a[0])-Number(b[0]))*111320*Math.cos(lat),(Number(a[1])-Number(b[1]))*110540);
}
function earthlineMedian16398(vals){
  const a=(vals||[]).map(Number).filter(Number.isFinite).sort((x,y)=>x-y);if(!a.length)return null;const m=Math.floor(a.length/2);return a.length%2?a[m]:(a[m-1]+a[m])/2;
}

function drawUSGSAquifers(){
  if(!M.usgsAquifers.length||earthlineNativeAquiferActive())return;
  const z=earthlineMapActive()&&earthlineMap.getZoom?earthlineMap.getZoom():Number(M.viewZoom||12);
  // At town/valley overview scale the aquifer must read instantly. Closer in, reduce
  // opacity and line weight so the imagery remains usable for selecting land.

  const fillAlpha=overview?0.48:0.27;
  const outerWidth=overview?17:10;
  const solidWidth=overview?8:5.5;

    ctx.save();ctx.strokeStyle="rgba(58,27,0,0.88)";ctx.lineJoin="round";ctx.lineWidth=outerWidth+5;ctx.stroke();ctx.restore();

    ctx.save();ctx.strokeStyle="#ff9f16";ctx.lineJoin="round";ctx.lineWidth=solidWidth;ctx.stroke();ctx.restore();
    ctx.save();ctx.strokeStyle="rgba(255,238,205,0.98)";ctx.lineJoin="round";ctx.lineWidth=innerWidth;ctx.stroke();ctx.restore();
  });

  const dpr=window.devicePixelRatio||1;

const G2S=(x,y)=>{
  if(earthlineMapActive()&&!earthlineUseFastPropertyProjection){
    const p=earthlineMap.project(earthlineGridToLngLat(x,y));return[p.x,p.y];
  }
  return[view.ox+x*view.s,view.oy+y*view.s];
};
const S2G=(x,y)=>{
  if(earthlineMapActive()){
    const ll=earthlineMap.unproject([x,y]);return lngLatToGrid(ll.lng,ll.lat);
  }
  return[(x-view.ox)/view.s,(y-view.oy)/view.s];
};

<section class="el49-page"><div class="el49-kicker">05 · Opinions and Interpretation</div><h2>Professional interpretation of the modeled findings</h2><div class="el49-grid"><div class="el49-panel"><h3>Relative priority</h3><p>A-grade corridors are the highest-priority opportunities within this run—not globally and not by construction feasibility. Land tenure, ownership, governance and implementation access do not erase the physical finding; they affect whether and how a project proceeds.</p></div></div><div class="el49-callout warn"><b>Opinion boundary:</b> no net recharge volume, flood-peak reduction, erosion reduction, carbon sequestration or biodiversity gain is quantified unless its required input chain is present and stated.</div>${footer(data,'05')}</section>

<section class="el49-page"><div class="el49-kicker">09 · Limitations and Reliance</div><h2>Conditions of use</h2><ul><li>This report presents screening results generated by the Earthline search engine from remotely sensed terrain, mapped contextual data and rule-based hydrologic screening. It is not an engineering design, construction document, geotechnical investigation, survey, permit, environmental assessment, title opinion or physical site assessment.</li><li>Regional and Focus outputs are opportunity corridors or candidate reaches. Exact swale locations are prohibited above Property tier.</li><li>Terrain, hydrography, soils, rainfall, land cover, exclusions and aquifer evidence carry independent source versions and limitations.</li><li>Camera-scoped NOAA GRACE and WRI Aqueduct rasters are visual context only and are excluded from report findings.</li><li>Absence of mapped aquifer geometry is never interpreted as absence of groundwater.</li><li>Pre-DTM corridor-specific drawdown verdicts, storage, earthworks and recharge estimates remain provisional because corridor position and contributing area may change.</li><li>Qualified local professionals and field verification are required before implementation.</li></ul><div class="el49-callout"><b>Reliance.</b> Earthline search-engine results may support screening, prioritization and scoping of further work. It may not be relied upon for construction, regulatory compliance, financing-grade engineering quantities or performance guarantees.</div><h3 style="margin-top:24px">Machine provenance</h3><table class="el49-table"><tbody><tr><th>Build</th><td>${esc(BUILD)}</td></tr><tr><th>Run token</th><td class="el49-source">${esc(d.runToken||'not recorded')}</td></tr><tr><th>Bounds hash</th><td class="el49-source">${esc(d.boundsHash||'not recorded')}</td></tr><tr><th>Completed</th><td>${esc(d.completedAt||'not recorded')}</td></tr><tr><th>Report generated</th><td>${esc(new Date().toISOString())}</td></tr></tbody></table>${footer(data,'09')}</section>

function earthlineSetMapView(loc){
  if(!earthlineMap)return;
  const apply=()=>{
    const planning=earthlineLargeAreaMode()||earthlinePlanningMode();
    const lng=Number(loc&&loc.lng);
    const lat=Number(loc&&loc.lat);
    const center=[Number.isFinite(lng)?lng:Number(M.centerLng||0),Number.isFinite(lat)?lat:Number(M.centerLat||0)];
    const cameraToken=++earthlineSearchCameraToken;
    try{
      earthlineMap.stop();
      earthlineClearTarget();
      earthlineMap.setProjection({name:"globe"});
      if(earthlineMap.setFog)earthlineMap.setFog(null);
      // Regional terrain ownership belongs to the live regional controller. Do not
      // null the terrain mesh here; doing so made queryTerrainElevation fail mid-run.
      earthlineMap.scrollZoom.enable();
      earthlineMap.dragPan.enable();earthlineMap.doubleClickZoom.enable();earthlineMap.keyboard.disable();
      earthlineMap.touchZoomRotate.enable();earthlineMap.touchZoomRotate.disableRotation();
      earthlineMap.dragRotate.disable();
      const base=document.getElementById("mapboxBase");
      if(base)base.classList.remove("globe-overview");
      earthlineMap.resize();
    }catch(e){}

    let targetZoom=(loc&&Number.isFinite(Number(loc.zoomHint)))?Number(loc.zoomHint):(M.viewZoom||17);
    if(loc&&Array.isArray(loc.bbox)&&loc.bbox.length===4&&planning){
      try{
        const cam=earthlineMap.cameraForBounds([[loc.bbox[0],loc.bbox[1]],[loc.bbox[2],loc.bbox[3]]],{padding:52,maxZoom:11});
        if(cam&&Number.isFinite(cam.zoom))targetZoom=cam.zoom;
      }catch(e){}
    }
    targetZoom=Math.max(0,Math.min(19,targetZoom));

    // Reset any persistent camera padding, then hold the searched coordinate at the
    // geometric center while laptop layout, fonts, panels and map tiles settle.
    // The lock cancels immediately when the user starts navigating.
    const started=performance.now();
    earthlineSearchCameraLock={token:cameraToken,center,zoom:targetZoom,started,expires:started+1500};
    earthlineApplySearchCameraLock(true);
    requestAnimationFrame(()=>requestAnimationFrame(()=>earthlineApplySearchCameraLock()));
    [120,320,700,1200].forEach(ms=>setTimeout(()=>earthlineApplySearchCameraLock(),ms));
    earthlineMap.once("idle",()=>earthlineApplySearchCameraLock());

    earthlineUpdateGlobePresentation();
    earthlineSyncOverlayScale();
    if(earthlineOverlayNeeded())earthlineScheduleOverlayRender();
    else earthlineClearOverlay();
  };
  if(earthlineMapReady)apply(); else earthlineMap.once("load",apply);
}

    }
  }

  const nearest=[];
  for(let i=0;i<nearestByCorridor.length;i++){
    const best=nearestByCorridor[i];
    if(Number.isFinite(best))nearest.push(best);
  }
  nearest.sort((a,b)=>a-b);
  const mid=Math.floor(nearest.length/2);
  const median=nearest.length%2?nearest[mid]:(nearest[mid-1]+nearest[mid])/2;
  const mean=nearest.reduce((a,b)=>a+b,0)/Math.max(1,nearest.length);
  return{sampleCount:nearest.length,medianM:median,meanM:mean,minM:nearest[0]||0,maxM:nearest[nearest.length-1]||0};
}

  }

        const s=(e-M.fill[idx(nx,ny)])/D8D[d];
        if(s>bestS){bestS=s;best=d;}
      }
      M.dir[i]=best;
    }
    if((y&7)===7){earthlineAssertActive15686(myGen);await earthlineCoopYield15686(myGen,"hydrology");}
  }
  const order=new Int32Array(N);
  for(let i=0;i<N;i++)order[i]=i;
  const f=M.fill;
  // Sorting 30k cells is acceptable, but yield before and after so the UI can repaint.
  earthlineAssertActive15686(myGen);await earthlineCoopYield15686(myGen,"hydrology");
  Array.prototype.sort.call(order,(a,b)=>f[b]-f[a]);
  earthlineAssertActive15686(myGen);await earthlineCoopYield15686(myGen,"hydrology");
  M.acc.fill(1);
  let maxAcc=1,outFlow=0;
  for(let k=0;k<N;k++){
    const i=order[k],d=M.dir[i];
    if(d<0){if(M.inP[i])outFlow+=M.acc[i];continue;}
    const x=i%GW,y=(i/GW)|0;
    const ni=idx(x+D8X[d],y+D8Y[d]);
    M.acc[ni]+=M.acc[i];
    if(M.inP[i]&&!M.inP[ni])outFlow+=M.acc[i];
    if(M.acc[ni]>maxAcc)maxAcc=M.acc[ni];
    if((k&2047)===2047){earthlineAssertActive15686(myGen);await earthlineCoopYield15686(myGen,"hydrology");}
  }
  M.maxAcc=maxAcc;M.outFlow=outFlow;
}

/* ---------------- thresholds, census, helpers ---------------- */
function streamThreshold(){
  const rainF=1-(M.rain-5)/95*0.85;          // heavier rain reveals more rills
  let base=Math.max(2,M.maxAcc*0.005*rainF);
  // At regional scale the coarse DEM makes D8 produce straight grid-locked tributary
  // artifacts on near-flat ground. Raise the threshold sharply so only MAJOR rivers
  // (high real accumulation) render — these are genuine and not grid artifacts.
  if(!swaleDetailScale()){                    // zoomed out to watershed/regional
    base=Math.max(base,M.maxAcc*0.06);        // only the strongest channels
  }
  return base;
}
function censusTributaries(thr){
  let sources=0,lenCells=0;
  for(let y=0;y<GH;y++)for(let x=0;x<GW;x++){
    const i=idx(x,y);
    if(M.acc[i]<thr||!M.inP[i])continue;
    lenCells++;
    let hasUp=false;
    for(let d=0;d<8;d++){
      const nx=x+D8X[d],ny=y+D8Y[d];
      if(nx<0||ny<0||nx>=GW||ny>=GH)continue;
      const ni=idx(nx,ny);
      if(M.acc[ni]>=thr&&M.dir[ni]>=0){
        const dx=nx+D8X[M.dir[ni]],dy=ny+D8Y[M.dir[ni]];
        if(dx===x&&dy===y){hasUp=true;break;}
      }
    }
    if(!hasUp)sources++;
  }
  return {sources,lenCells};
}
function gradAt(x,y){
  const x0=clamp(x-1,0,GW-1),x1=clamp(x+1,0,GW-1);
  const y0=clamp(y-1,0,GH-1),y1=clamp(y+1,0,GH-1);
  const gx=(M.h[idx(x1,y)]-M.h[idx(x0,y)])/((x1-x0)*M.cellM);
  const gy=(M.h[idx(x,y1)]-M.h[idx(x,y0)])/((y1-y0)*M.cellM);
  return [gx,gy];
}
// bilinear elevation at fractional cell (x,y), in the same units as M.h (meters)
function hAt(x,y){
  const x0=clamp(Math.floor(x),0,GW-1),y0=clamp(Math.floor(y),0,GH-1);
  const x1=clamp(x0+1,0,GW-1),y1=clamp(y0+1,0,GH-1);
  const fx=x-x0,fy=y-y0;
  const h00=M.h[idx(x0,y0)],h10=M.h[idx(x1,y0)],h01=M.h[idx(x0,y1)],h11=M.h[idx(x1,y1)];
  return h00*(1-fx)*(1-fy)+h10*fx*(1-fy)+h01*(1-fx)*fy+h11*fx*fy;
}
// gradient in elevation-per-CELL (not per-meter), for the contour corrector working in cell space
function gradCell(x,y){
  const x0=clamp(Math.round(x)-1,0,GW-1),x1=clamp(Math.round(x)+1,0,GW-1);
  const y0=clamp(Math.round(y)-1,0,GH-1),y1=clamp(Math.round(y)+1,0,GH-1);
  const gx=(M.h[idx(x1,Math.round(y))]-M.h[idx(x0,Math.round(y))])/(x1-x0||1);
  const gy=(M.h[idx(Math.round(x),y1)]-M.h[idx(Math.round(x),y0)])/(y1-y0||1);
  return [gx,gy];
}

    const key=String(M.searchGen||0)+"|"+String(M.terrainStamp||"")+"|"+String(M.cellM||0)+"|"+String(M.maxAcc||0);
    if(!earthlineSlopeMemo16288||earthlineSlopeMemo16288.length!==N||earthlineSlopeMemoKey16288!==key){
      earthlineSlopeMemo16288=new Float64Array(N);earthlineSlopeMemo16288.fill(NaN);earthlineSlopeMemoKey16288=key;
    }

/* ---------------- bioswale prescription ---------------- */
// Sample the loaded satellite imagery to estimate tree canopy at a grid cell.
// Forest = dark, saturated green, high texture. Open ground (grass/soil/roof/dry)
// is lighter, less saturated. Returns 0 (open) .. 1 (dense canopy).
let _canopyData=null,_canopyW=0,_canopyH=0,_impervData=null,_satRgbaData=null;
function buildCanopyField(){
  _canopyData=null;_impervData=null;_satRgbaData=null;
  if(!(M.realMode&&M.satCanvas))return;          // only meaningful on real imagery
  try{
    const sc=M.satCanvas, sw=sc.width, sh=sc.height;
    const id=sc.getContext("2d").getImageData(0,0,sw,sh).data;
    _satRgbaData=id;
    _canopyW=sw;_canopyH=sh;_canopyData=new Float32Array(sw*sh);
    _impervData=new Float32Array(sw*sh);
    for(let p=0,q=0;p<id.length;p+=4,q++){
      const r=id[p],g=id[p+1],b=id[p+2];
      // Forest reads as dark, green-dominant. Open ground (grass/dry/soil/roof/road)
      // is brighter and/or red-leaning. Build a robust 0..1 canopy score.
      const green=g-(r+b)/2;                 // green dominance
      const lum=(r+g+b)/3;
      // TRUE forest canopy = dark AND strongly green together. Open sage/grass is
      // mid-tone and only weakly green, and must NOT register as forest.
      let c=0;
      const isDark=lum<95;                    // genuinely shaded canopy
      const strongGreen=green>22;             // clearly green-dominant (not pale sage)
      if(isDark && green>12) c=0.55+Math.min(0.4,(green-12)/40);  // dark + green = tree
      else if(strongGreen && lum<140) c=0.45+Math.min(0.4,(green-22)/40); // lush green
      else c=0;                                // bright, pale, dry, or red = open ground
      if(lum>160)c=0;                          // anything bright = open, hard rule
      if(r>=g)c=0;                             // red/tan-leaning = dry open ground
      _canopyData[q]=Math.max(0,Math.min(1,c));
      // IMPERVIOUS (roof/pavement/concrete/asphalt): man-made surfaces are low-saturation
      // (grey/blue-grey) and NOT green vegetation, across a wide brightness range — bright
      // concrete, mid pavement, AND dark shingle/asphalt roofs.
      const mx=Math.max(r,g,b), mn=Math.min(r,g,b);
      const sat=mx?(mx-mn)/mx:0;              // colour saturation 0..1
      let imp=0;
      if(green<14 && c<0.25){                 // exclude vegetation/canopy
        // Man-made surfaces: low-to-moderate saturation greys across brightness. We bias
        // toward catching roofs even at some risk of flagging a little bare ground, because
        // a swale stopping short of a roof is FAR safer than one drawn across a house.
        if(lum>160 && sat<0.20) imp=0.75;                              // bright concrete/roof
        else if(lum>=55 && lum<=160 && sat<0.18) imp=0.62;            // grey/warm-grey roof
        else if(b>=r && b>g-2 && lum>70 && sat<0.32) imp=0.6;         // blue-grey metal roof
        else if(lum>=50 && lum<=120 && sat<0.22) imp=0.55;           // dark shingle/asphalt
      }
      _impervData[q]=Math.max(0,Math.min(1,imp));
    }
  }catch(e){_canopyData=null;_impervData=null;_satRgbaData=null;}   // CORS taint etc -> skip gracefully
}
// canopy at grid cell (gx,gy) by mapping grid -> satCanvas pixel space
function canopyAt(gx,gy){
  if(!_canopyData)return 0;
  const px=Math.round((gx/GW)*_canopyW), py=Math.round((gy/GH)*_canopyH);
  let sum=0,nn=0;
  for(let oy=-2;oy<=2;oy++)for(let ox=-2;ox<=2;ox++){
    const x=px+ox,y=py+oy;
    if(x<0||y<0||x>=_canopyW||y>=_canopyH)continue;
    sum+=_canopyData[y*_canopyW+x];nn++;
  }
  return nn?sum/nn:0;
}

  let sum=0,nn=0;
  for(let oy=-2;oy<=2;oy++)for(let ox=-2;ox<=2;ox++){
    const x=pxr+ox,y=pyr+oy;
    if(x<0||y<0||

    suitabilityScoresChanged:false,
    at:new Date().toISOString()
  };
}

function earthlineBridgeUsable(a,b,maxS){
  const gapCells=Math.hypot(b[0]-a[0],b[1]-a[1]);

function earthlineJoinPathsIfCompatible(a,b,maxS,maxGapM){
  if(!a||!b||a.length<2||b.length<2)return null;
  const variants=[
    [a,b],
    [a,b.slice().reverse()],
    [a.slice().reverse(),b],
    [a.slice().reverse(),b.slice().reverse()]
  ];
  let best=null;
  for(const pair of variants){
    const pa=pair[0],pb=pair[1],ae=pa[pa.length-1],bs=pb[0];
    const gapM=Math.hypot(bs[0]-ae[0],bs[1]-ae[1])*M.cellM;
    if(gapM>maxGapM)continue;
    const ta=earthlineVecUnit(pa[pa.length-2][0],pa[pa.length-2][1],ae[0],ae[1]);
    const tb=earthlineVecUnit(bs[0],bs[1],pb[1][0],pb[1][1]);
    const bridge=earthlineVecUnit(ae[0],ae[1],bs[0],bs[1]);
    // A contour can curve, but a join should continue generally forward rather than
    // doubling back into a hook or connecting unrelated parallel lines.
    // 15624: a join must continue in the same general direction. The previous permissive
    // threshold allowed hook and near-90-degree connections that did not resemble a level swale.
    const minForward=gapM<=Math.max(2.5,M.cellM*2.5)?0.48:0.68;
    const aligned=earthlineDot2(ta,bridge)>minForward&&
      earthlineDot2(bridge,tb)>minForward&&earthlineDot2(ta,tb)>0.50;
    if(!aligned||!earthlineBridgeUsable(ae,bs,maxS))continue;
    const gapCells=Math.hypot(bs[0]-ae[0],bs[1]-ae[1]);
    const n=Math.max(1,Math.ceil(gapCells));
    const bridgePts=[];
    for(let k=1;k<n;k++){
      const t=k/n;bridgePts.push([ae[0]+(bs[0]-ae[0])*t,ae[1]+(bs[1]-ae[1])*t]);
    }
    const joined=earthlineCleanPath(pa.concat(bridgePts,pb));
    const rank=gapM+(1-earthlineDot2(ta,tb))*2;
    if(!best||rank<best.rank)best={pts:joined,rank};
  }
  return best?best.pts:null;
}
function earthlineMergeCompatibleSwales(swales,chanThr,maxS,totalBands,targetM){
  const pool=(swales||[]).slice().sort((a,b)=>(a.band-b.band)||(b.lenM-a.lenM));
  const used=new Uint8Array(pool.length),out=[];
  const maxGapM=Math.min(8,Math.max(4,targetM*0.65));
  for(let i=0;i<pool.length;i++){
    if(used[i])continue;
    used[i]=1;
    let current=pool[i],changed=true,passes=0;
    while(changed&&passes++<6){
      changed=false;let bestJ=-1,bestPts=null,bestLen=0;
      for(let j=0;j<pool.length;j++){
        if(used[j]||pool[j].band!==current.band)continue;
        const joined=earthlineJoinPathsIfCompatible(current.pts,pool[j].pts,maxS,maxGapM);
        if(!joined)continue;
        const len=earthlineSegmentLengthM(joined);
        if(len>bestLen){bestLen=len;bestJ=j;bestPts=joined;}
      }
      if(bestJ>=0){
        const other=pool[bestJ];
        const rebuilt=earthlineSwaleFromPts(bestPts,current.band,chanThr,current.isSite||other.isSite,maxS,totalBands);
        if(rebuilt){
          rebuilt.merged=true;
          current=rebuilt;used[bestJ]=1;changed=true;
        }
      }
    }
    out.push(current);
  }
  return out;
}

  for(const pair of variants){
    const pa=pair[0],pb=pair[1],ae=pa[pa.length-1],bs=pb[0];
    const gapM=Math.hypot(bs[0]-ae[0],bs[1]-ae[1])*M.cellM;
    if(gapM>maxGapM)continue;
    const ta=earthlineVecUnit(pa[pa.length-2][0],pa[pa.length-2][1],ae[0],ae[1]);
    const tb=earthlineVecUnit(bs[0],bs[1],pb[1][0],pb[1][1]);
    const bridge=earthlineVecUnit(ae[0],ae[1],bs[0],bs[1]);
    // A contour can curve, but a join should continue generally forward rather than
    // doubling back into a hook or connecting unrelated parallel lines.
    // 15624: a join must continue in the same general direction. The previous permissive
    // threshold allowed hook and near-90-degree connections that did not resemble a level swale.
    const minForward=gapM<=Math.max(2.5,M.cellM*2.5)?0.48:0.68;
    const aligned=earthlineDot2(ta,bridge)>minForward&&
      earthlineDot2(bridge,tb)>minForward&&earthlineDot2(ta,tb)>0.50;
    if(!aligned||!earthlineBridgeUsable(ae,bs,maxS))continue;
    const gapCells=Math.hypot(bs[0]-ae[0],bs[1]-ae[1]);
    const n=Math.max(1,Math.ceil(gapCells));
    const bridgePts=[];
    for(let k=1;k<n;k++){
      const t=k/n;bridgePts.push([ae[0]+(bs[0]-ae[0])*t,ae[1]+(bs[1]-ae[1])*t]);
    }
    const joined=earthlineCleanPath(pa.concat(bridgePts,pb));
    const rank=gapM+(1-earthlineDot2(ta,tb))*2;
    if(!best||rank<best.rank)best={pts:joined,rank};
  }
  return best?best.pts:null;
}
function earthlineMergeCompatibleSwales(swales,chanThr,maxS,totalBands,targetM){
  const pool=(swales||[]).slice().sort((a,b)=>(a.band-b.band)||(b.lenM-a.lenM));
  const used=new Uint8Array(pool.length),out=[];
  const maxGapM=Math.min(8,Math.max(4,targetM*0.65));
  for(let i=0;i<pool.length;i++){
    if(used[i])continue;
    used[i]=1;
    let current=pool[i],changed=true,passes=0;
    while(changed&&passes++<6){
      changed=false;let bestJ=-1,bestPts=null,bestLen=0;
      for(let j=0;j<pool.length;j++){
        if(used[j]||pool[j].band!==current.band)continue;
        const joined=earthlineJoinPathsIfCompatible(current.pts,pool[j].pts,maxS,maxGapM);
        if(!joined)continue;
        const len=earthlineSegmentLengthM(joined);
        if(len>bestLen){bestLen=len;bestJ=j;bestPts=joined;}
      }
      if(bestJ>=0){
        const other=pool[bestJ];
        const rebuilt=earthlineSwaleFromPts(bestPts,current.band,chanThr,current.isSite||other.isSite,maxS,totalBands);
        if(rebuilt){
          rebuilt.merged=true;
          current=rebuilt;used[bestJ]=1;changed=true;
        }
      }
    }
    out.push(current);
  }
  return out;
}

function earthlineSwaleGeometryAcceptable(pts,lenM){
  if(!pts||pts.length<3||lenM<12)return false;
  const a=pts[0],b=pts[pts.length-1],gapM=Math.hypot(a[0]-b[0],a[1]-b[1])*M.cellM;
  let minX=Infinity,maxX=-Infinity,minY=Infinity,maxY=-Infinity;
  for(const p of pts){minX=Math.min(minX,p[0]);maxX=Math.max(maxX,p[0]);minY=Math.min(minY,p[1]);maxY=Math.max(maxY,p[1]);}
  const diagM=Math.hypot(maxX-minX,maxY-minY)*M.cellM;
  const loopish=gapM<Math.max(8,lenM*0.12);
  // Suppress small closed rings and compact bullseyes caused by neighborhood/DEM artifacts.
  if(loopish&&(lenM<240||lenM/Math.max(1,diagM)>3.2))return false;
  if(lenM/Math.max(1,diagM)>6)return false;
  return true;
}

}

function drawUSGSAquifers(){
  if(!M.usgsAquifers.length||earthlineNativeAquiferActive())return;
  const z=earthlineMapActive()&&earthlineMap.getZoom?earthlineMap.getZoom():Number(M.viewZoom||12);
  // At town/valley overview scale the aquifer must read instantly. Closer in, reduce
  // opacity and line weight so the imagery remains usable for selecting land.
  const overview=z<=12.8;
  const fillAlpha=overview?0.48:0.27;
  const outerWidth=overview?17:10;
  const solidWidth=overview?8:5.5;
  const innerWidth=overview?2.4:1.7;
  const glowBlur=overview?28:17;

  ctx.save();
  M.usgsAquifers.forEach(poly=>{
    ctx.beginPath();
    poly.pts.forEach((p,i)=>{const[ax,ay]=G2S(p[0],p[1]);if(i===0)ctx.moveTo(ax,ay);else ctx.lineTo(ax,ay);});
    ctx.closePath();

    // Solid warm fill makes the aquifer footprint unmistakable against satellite imagery.
    ctx.fillStyle=`rgba(255,145,0,${fillAlpha})`;ctx.fill();

    // Dark backing edge prevents the orange perimeter from disappearing over pale roads/rock.
    ctx.save();ctx.strokeStyle="rgba(58,27,0,0.88)";ctx.lineJoin="round";ctx.lineWidth=outerWidth+5;ctx.stroke();ctx.restore();

    // Restrained orange halo + solid border: prominent at high elevation, not neon.
    ctx.save();ctx.shadowColor="rgba(255,132,0,0.92)";ctx.shadowBlur=glowBlur;ctx.strokeStyle="rgba(255,126,0,0.98)";ctx.lineJoin="round";ctx.lineWidth=outerWidth;ctx.stroke();ctx.restore();
    ctx.save();ctx.strokeStyle="#ff9f16";ctx.lineJoin="round";ctx.lineWidth=solidWidth;ctx.stroke();ctx.restore();
    ctx.save();ctx.strokeStyle="rgba(255,238,205,0.98)";ctx.lineJoin="round";ctx.lineWidth=innerWidth;ctx.stroke();ctx.restore();
  });

  // Light interior hatch helps users distinguish an aquifer polygon from a general heatmap.
  ctx.save();
  M.usgsAquifers.forEach(poly=>{
    ctx.beginPath();
    poly.pts.forEach((p,i)=>{const[ax,ay]=G2S(p[0],p[1]);if(i===0)ctx.moveTo(ax,ay);else ctx.lineTo(ax,ay);});
    ctx.closePath();ctx.clip();
    let minx=1e9,miny=1e9,maxx=-1e9,maxy=-1e9;
    poly.pts.forEach(p=>{const[ax,ay]=G2S(p[0],p[1]);minx=Math.min(minx,ax);miny=Math.min(miny,ay);maxx=Math.max(maxx,ax);maxy=Math.max(maxy,ay);});
    ctx.strokeStyle=overview?"rgba(255,230,175,0.44)":"rgba(255,226,168,0.28)";
    ctx.lineWidth=overview?1.5:1;
    const step=overview?15:18;
    for(let d=minx-(maxy-miny);d<maxx;d+=step){ctx.beginPath();ctx.moveTo(d,miny);ctx.lineTo(d+(maxy-miny),maxy);ctx.stroke();}
  });
  ctx.restore();

  const seen=new Set();
  M.usgsAquifers.forEach(poly=>{
    if(seen.has(poly.name))return;seen.add(poly.name);
    let cx=0,cy=0;poly.pts.forEach(p=>{cx+=p[0];cy+=p[1];});cx/=poly.pts.length;cy/=poly.pts.length;
    const[sx,sy]=G2S(cx,cy);
    const label=(overview?"AQUIFER · ":"")+poly.name;
    ctx.font=(overview?"800 13px":"700 12px")+" Segoe UI, sans-serif";
    ctx.textAlign="center";ctx.textBaseline="middle";
    const tw=ctx.measureText(label).width;
    if(overview){
      ctx.fillStyle="rgba(72,32,0,0.88)";
      ctx.fillRect(sx-tw/2-8,sy-11,tw+16,22);
      ctx.strokeStyle="rgba(255,184,72,0.95)";ctx.lineWidth=1.2;
      ctx.strokeRect(sx-tw/2-8,sy-11,tw+16,22);
    }
    ctx.lineWidth=overview?4.5:3.5;ctx.strokeStyle="rgba(43,18,0,0.96)";ctx.strokeText(label,sx,sy);
    ctx.fillStyle="#ffd58d";ctx.fillText(label,sx,sy);
  });
  ctx.restore();
}


// BUILD-CHECK-15672: Mapbox aquifer polygons live below the analytical canvas.
// This restrained outline/hatch pass keeps official aquifers legible over bright,
// high-elevation satellite terrain and over flow/recharge overlays. Swales are drawn
// afterward, so the design recommendations remain the top visual priority.
function earthlineAquiferScreenRing(poly){
  const ll=(poly&&poly.lngLatPts)||[];
  if(earthlineMapActive()&&ll.length>=3){
    return ll.map(c=>{
      const p=earthlineMap.project({lng:Number(c[0]),lat:Number(c[1])});
      return [p.x,p.y];
    }).filter(p=>Number.isFinite(p[0])&&Number.isFinite(p[1]));
  }
  return ((poly&&poly.pts)||[]).map(p=>G2S(p[0],p[1]));
}
function earthlineTraceScreenRing(ring){
  if(!ring||ring.length<3)return false;
  ctx.beginPath();
  ring.forEach((p,i)=>i?ctx.lineTo(p[0],p[1]):ctx.moveTo(p[0],p[1]));
  ctx.closePath();
  return true;
}

  function currentMapSite(){
    if(!earthlineMapActive()||!earthlineMap||!earthlineMap.getCenter)return null;

    const z=earthlineMap.getZoom?earthlineMap.getZoom():Number(M.viewZoom||0);
    const c=earthlineMap.getCenter();
    if(!c||!Number.isFinite(c.lng)||!Number.isFinite(c.lat))return null;

    // BUILD-CHECK-15760:
    // Run Analysis always uses the map's CURRENT center at button press.
    // It never reuses the original geocode point after the user pans.
    const parent=M.loc||{};
    const pt=String(parent.placeType||'').toLowerCase();

    // Property mode is reserved for an exact address or an explicitly drawn/selected boundary.
    const hasExplicitBoundary=Array.isArray(M.analysisBoundaryLngLat)&&M.analysisBoundaryLngLat.length>=3;
    const exactProperty=(pt==='address')||hasExplicitBoundary;

    let acres;
    let mode;
    let analysisZoom;

    if(exactProperty){
      mode='property';
      acres=Math.max(8,Math.min(80,Number(parent.acres||20)));
      analysisZoom=Math.max(16,Math.min(18,Number.isFinite(z)?z:17));
    }else{
      mode='regional';

      // Wider opportunity search for general locations, POIs, open land, hillsides,
      // places "behind Walmart", forests, valleys, watersheds, etc.
      // BUILD-CHECK-15760: one real-engine regional tile at a time. 320 acres keeps
      // the 210×146 analysis grid at approximately 15–25 ft per cell instead of
      // pretending a multi-thousand-acre frame can support property-scale spacing.
      acres=320;
      analysisZoom=16.6;
    }

    const rect=(typeof earthlineRectangleAround==='function')
      ? earthlineRectangleAround(c.lng,c.lat,acres)
      : [];

    return {
      name:mode==='regional'?'Regional Swale Opportunity Area':'Selected Property Analysis Area',
      sub:(mode==='regional'
        ? 'Broad opportunity search centered on the current map view near '
        : 'Property-scale analysis centered on the current map view near ')
        +((parent&&parent.name)||'searched location'),
      fullName:mode==='regional'?'Regional swale opportunity area':'Selected property analysis area',
      lat:c.lat,
      lng:c.lng,
      zoomHint:analysisZoom,
      placeType:mode==='regional'?'regional_opportunity':'selected_site',
      userSelectedSite:true,
      planning:false,
      acres,
      regionalMode:mode==='regional',
      propertyMode:mode==='property',
      elevM:Number.isFinite(Number(parent.elevM))?Number(parent.elevM):0,
      trendDeg:Number(parent.trendDeg||180),
      trendSlope:Number(parent.trendSlope||6),
      relief:Number(parent.relief||8),
      rough:Number(parent.rough||1),
      runC:Number(parent.runC||0.35),
      precipIn:Number(parent.precipIn||30),
      zones:[mode==='regional'?'Regional Opportunity Area':'Selected Property'],
      analysisBoundaryLngLat:rect
    };
  }

n<li><strong>Snowmelt runoff ratios averaged 21% at uncultivated grassland sites against 43% at cultivated sites.</strong> In a more humid climate closer to Vermont's, the split was <strong>52% versus 72%</strong>. Nitrogen available for leaching is directly related to impervious cover in the contributing watershed.</p>\n<p><strong>Vadose zone thickness.</strong> Upslope positions generally have a deeper unsaturated zone. Chloride concentrations in runoff are <strong>highest in mid-to-late winter and early spring</strong>, from road de-icing, and often exceed both surface and groundwater standards. Nitrate behaves similarly: highly soluble, staying in solution after leaving the root zone until it reaches groundwater.</p>\n<p>Soil depth does not help with either.</p>\n<p><strong>A swale intercepting salted road runoff is not a recharge asset. It is an injection pathway to the aquifer.</strong></p>\n<p>This couples badly with §6.1 and §6.3: macropore flow in frozen soil <strong>facilitates preferential contaminant transport to aquifers</strong>, and the peak chloride window is the snowmelt window. Vermont's largest recharge opportunity and its highest contamination risk are the same event, moving through the same pathway.</p>\n<p><strong>The resulting rule is simple.</strong> Maximise root-driven infiltration where the contributing area is clean. Never site for infiltration where it is not. Speed and treatment trade against each other, and position on the slope decides which one is needed.</p>\n<hr />\n","<h3>6.5 · Soil generation and reforestation</h3>\n<p>A swale is the only water structure that manufactures its own growing medium.</p>\n<p><strong>The sequence compounds.</strong> Re-watered ground supports planting.
